- **idea-evaluation**: Converges a set of ideas to a ranked, defensible few: pressure-tests each idea's premise against reality, scores on feasibility, value, fit, cost and risk for the real actor, identifies the assumption each idea most depends on and the cheapest way to test it, and recommends with reasoning and next steps.
